   24 | entity neorv32_cpu_control is
   25 |   generic (
   27 |     HART_ID           : natural range 0 to 1023;

   30 |     DEBUG_EXC_ADDR    : std_ulogic_vector(31 downto 0);

   42 |     RISCV_ISA_Zbkb    : boolean;

   68 |   port (
   70 |     clk_i         : in  std_ulogic;
   71 |     rstn_i        : in  std_ulogic;

   76 |     pmp_fault_i   : in  std_ulogic;
   78 |     alu_cp_done_i : in  std_ulogic;

   85 |     irq_dbg_i     : in  std_ulogic;
   86 |     irq_machine_i : in  std_ulogic_vector(2 downto 0);
   87 |     irq_fast_i    : in  std_ulogic_vector(15 downto 0);
   89 |     lsu_wait_i    : in  std_ulogic;
   90 |     lsu_mar_i     : in  std_ulogic_vector(XLEN-1 downto 0);
   91 |     lsu_err_i     : in  std_ulogic_vector(3 downto 0)
   92 |   );
   93 | end neorv32_cpu_control;

   95 | architecture neorv32_cpu_control_rtl of neorv32_cpu_control is
   98 |   type exe_engine_state_t is (EX_DISPATCH, EX_TRAP_ENTER, EX_TRAP_EXIT, EX_RESTART, EX_SLEEP, EX_EXECUTE,
   99 |                               EX_ALU_WAIT, EX_BRANCH, EX_BRANCHED, EX_SYSTEM, EX_MEM_REQ, EX_MEM_RSP);
  100 |   type exe_engine_t is record
  101 |     state : exe_engine_state_t;
  102 |     ir    : std_ulogic_vector(31 downto 0);
  103 |     ci    : std_ulogic;
  104 |     pc    : std_ulogic_vector(XLEN-1 downto 0);
  105 |     pc2   : std_ulogic_vector(XLEN-1 downto 0);
  106 |     ra    : std_ulogic_vector(XLEN-1 downto 0);
  107 |   end record;
  108 |   signal exe_engine, exe_engine_nxt : exe_engine_t;
  111 |   type trap_ctrl_t is record
  112 |     exc_buf     : std_ulogic_vector(exc_width_c-1 downto 0);
  113 |     exc_fire    : std_ulogic;
  114 |     irq_pnd     : std_ulogic_vector(irq_width_c-1 downto 0);
  115 |     irq_buf     : std_ulogic_vector(irq_width_c-1 downto 0);
  116 |     irq_fire    : std_ulogic_vector(2 downto 0);
  117 |     cause       : std_ulogic_vector(6 downto 0);
  118 |     epc         : std_ulogic_vector(XLEN-1 downto 0);
  120 |     env_pending : std_ulogic;
  121 |     env_enter   : std_ulogic;
  122 |     env_entered : std_ulogic;
  123 |     env_exit    : std_ulogic;
  124 |     wakeup      : std_ulogic;
  126 |     instr_be    : std_ulogic;
  127 |     instr_ma    : std_ulogic;
  128 |     instr_il    : std_ulogic;
  129 |     ecall       : std_ulogic;
  130 |     ebreak      : std_ulogic;
  131 |   end record;
  132 |   signal trap_ctrl : trap_ctrl_t;
  135 |   signal ctrl, ctrl_nxt : ctrl_bus_t;
  138 |   type csr_t is record
  139 |     addr           : std_ulogic_vector(11 downto 0);
  140 |     we, we_nxt     : std_ulogic;
  141 |     re, re_nxt     : std_ulogic;
  142 |     operand        : std_ulogic_vector(XLEN-1 downto 0);
  143 |     wdata          : std_ulogic_vector(XLEN-1 downto 0);
  144 |     rdata          : std_ulogic_vector(XLEN-1 downto 0);
  146 |     mstatus_mie    : std_ulogic;
  147 |     mstatus_mpie   : std_ulogic;
  148 |     mstatus_mpp    : std_ulogic;
  149 |     mstatus_mprv   : std_ulogic;
  150 |     mstatus_tw     : std_ulogic;
  152 |     mie_msi        : std_ulogic;
  153 |     mie_mei        : std_ulogic;
  154 |     mie_mti        : std_ulogic;
  155 |     mie_firq       : std_ulogic_vector(15 downto 0);
  157 |     prv_level      : std_ulogic;
  158 |     prv_level_eff  : std_ulogic;
  160 |     mepc           : std_ulogic_vector(XLEN-1 downto 0);
  161 |     mcause         : std_ulogic_vector(5 downto 0);
  162 |     mtvec          : std_ulogic_vector(XLEN-1 downto 0);
  163 |     mtval          : std_ulogic_vector(XLEN-1 downto 0);
  164 |     mtinst         : std_ulogic_vector(XLEN-1 downto 0);
  165 |     mscratch       : std_ulogic_vector(XLEN-1 downto 0);
  166 |     mcounteren_cy  : std_ulogic;
  167 |     mcounteren_ir  : std_ulogic;
  168 |     mcountinhibit  : std_ulogic_vector(15 downto 0);
  170 |     dcsr_ebreakm   : std_ulogic;
  171 |     dcsr_ebreaku   : std_ulogic;
  172 |     dcsr_step      : std_ulogic;
  173 |     dcsr_prv       : std_ulogic;
  174 |     dcsr_cause     : std_ulogic_vector(2 downto 0);
  175 |     dcsr_rd        : std_ulogic_vector(XLEN-1 downto 0);
  176 |     dpc            : std_ulogic_vector(XLEN-1 downto 0);
  177 |     dscratch0      : std_ulogic_vector(XLEN-1 downto 0);
  179 |     tdata1_execute : std_ulogic;
  180 |     tdata1_rd      : std_ulogic_vector(XLEN-1 downto 0);
  181 |     tdata2         : std_ulogic_vector(XLEN-1 downto 0);
  182 |   end record;
  183 |   signal csr : csr_t;
  186 |   type debug_ctrl_t is record
  187 |     run, trig_hw, trig_break, trig_halt, trig_step : std_ulogic;
  188 |   end record;
  189 |   signal debug_ctrl : debug_ctrl_t;
  192 |   signal if_ack       : std_ulogic;
  193 |   signal if_reset     : std_ulogic;
  194 |   signal branch_taken : std_ulogic;
  195 |   signal monitor_cnt  : std_ulogic_vector(monitor_mc_tmo_c downto 0);
  196 |   signal monitor_exc  : std_ulogic;
  197 |   signal opcode       : std_ulogic_vector(6 downto 0);
  198 |   signal immediate    : std_ulogic_vector(XLEN-1 downto 0);
  199 |   signal illegal_cmd  : std_ulogic;
  200 |   signal sleep_mode   : std_ulogic;
  201 |   signal csr_valid    : std_ulogic_vector(2 downto 0);
  202 |   signal cnt_event    : std_ulogic_vector(11 downto 0);
  203 |   signal hwtrig_match : std_ulogic;
  204 |   signal hwtrig_fired : std_ulogic;
  205 |   signal hwtrig_start : std_ulogic;
  207 | begin
  215 |   imm_gen: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
  216 |   begin
  217 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
  218 |       immediate <= (others => '0');
  219 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
  220 |       if (exe_engine.state = EX_DISPATCH) then
  221 |         if RISCV_ISA_C and (frontend_i.compr = '1') then
  222 |           immediate <= x"00000002";
  223 |         else
  224 |           immediate <= x"00000004";
  225 |         end if;
  226 |       else
  227 |         case opcode is
  228 |           when opcode_store_c =>
  229 |             immediate <= replicate_f(exe_engine.ir(31), 21) & exe_engine.ir(30 downto 25) & exe_engine.ir(11 downto 7);
  230 |           when opcode_branch_c =>
  231 |             immediate <= replicate_f(exe_engine.ir(31), 20) & exe_engine.ir(7) & exe_engine.ir(30 downto 25) & exe_engine.ir(11 downto 8) & '0';
  232 |           when opcode_lui_c | opcode_auipc_c =>
  233 |             immediate <= exe_engine.ir(31 downto 12) & x"000";
  234 |           when opcode_jal_c =>
  235 |             immediate <= replicate_f(exe_engine.ir(31), 12) & exe_engine.ir(19 downto 12) & exe_engine.ir(20) & exe_engine.ir(30 downto 21) & '0';
  236 |           when opcode_amo_c =>
  237 |             immediate <= (others => '0');
  238 |           when others =>
  239 |             immediate <= replicate_f(exe_engine.ir(31), 21) & exe_engine.ir(30 downto 21) & exe_engine.ir(20);
  240 |         end case;
  241 |       end if;
  242 |     end if;
  243 |   end process imm_gen;
  248 |   branch_check: process(exe_engine.ir, alu_cmp_i)
  249 |   begin
  250 |     if (exe_engine.ir(instr_opcode_lsb_c+2) = '0') then
  251 |       if (exe_engine.ir(instr_funct3_msb_c) = '0') then
  252 |         branch_taken <= alu_cmp_i(cmp_equal_c) xor exe_engine.ir(instr_funct3_lsb_c);
  253 |       else
  254 |         branch_taken <= alu_cmp_i(cmp_less_c) xor exe_engine.ir(instr_funct3_lsb_c);
  255 |       end if;
  256 |     else
  257 |       branch_taken <= '1';
  258 |     end if;
  259 |   end process branch_check;
  264 |   execute_engine_fsm_sync: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
  265 |   begin
  266 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
  267 |       ctrl             <= ctrl_bus_zero_c;
  268 |       exe_engine.state <= EX_RESTART;
  269 |       exe_engine.ir    <= (others => '0');
  270 |       exe_engine.ci    <= '0';
  271 |       exe_engine.pc    <= BOOT_ADDR(XLEN-1 downto 2) & "00";
  272 |       exe_engine.pc2   <= BOOT_ADDR(XLEN-1 downto 2) & "00";
  273 |       exe_engine.ra    <= (others => '0');
  274 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
  275 |       ctrl       <= ctrl_nxt;
  276 |       exe_engine <= exe_engine_nxt;
  277 |     end if;
  278 |   end process execute_engine_fsm_sync;
  281 |   opcode <= exe_engine.ir(instr_opcode_msb_c downto instr_opcode_lsb_c+2) & "11";
  286 |   execute_engine_fsm_comb: process(exe_engine, debug_ctrl, trap_ctrl, hwtrig_match, opcode, frontend_i, csr,
  287 |                                    ctrl, alu_cp_done_i, lsu_wait_i, alu_add_i, branch_taken, pmp_fault_i)
  288 |     variable funct3_v : std_ulogic_vector(2 downto 0);
  289 |     variable funct7_v : std_ulogic_vector(6 downto 0);
  290 |   begin
  292 |     funct3_v := exe_engine.ir(instr_funct3_msb_c downto instr_funct3_lsb_c);
  293 |     funct7_v := exe_engine.ir(instr_funct7_msb_c downto instr_funct7_lsb_c);
  296 |     exe_engine_nxt.state <= exe_engine.state;
  297 |     exe_engine_nxt.ir    <= exe_engine.ir;
  298 |     exe_engine_nxt.ci    <= exe_engine.ci;
  299 |     exe_engine_nxt.pc    <= exe_engine.pc;
  300 |     exe_engine_nxt.pc2   <= exe_engine.pc2;
  301 |     exe_engine_nxt.ra    <= (others => '0');
  302 |     if_ack               <= '0';
  303 |     if_reset             <= '0';
  304 |     trap_ctrl.env_enter  <= '0';
  305 |     trap_ctrl.env_exit   <= '0';
  306 |     trap_ctrl.instr_be   <= '0';
  307 |     trap_ctrl.instr_ma   <= '0';
  308 |     trap_ctrl.ecall      <= '0';
  309 |     trap_ctrl.ebreak     <= '0';
  310 |     csr.we_nxt           <= '0';
  311 |     csr.re_nxt           <= '0';
  312 |     ctrl_nxt             <= ctrl_bus_zero_c;
  313 |     hwtrig_start         <= '0';
  316 |     if (opcode(4) = '1') then
  317 |       ctrl_nxt.alu_unsigned <= funct3_v(0);
  318 |     else
  319 |       ctrl_nxt.alu_unsigned <= funct3_v(1);
  320 |     end if;
  323 |     case opcode is
  324 |       when opcode_auipc_c | opcode_jal_c | opcode_branch_c =>
  325 |         ctrl_nxt.alu_opa_mux <= '1';
  326 |       when others =>
  327 |         ctrl_nxt.alu_opa_mux <= '0';
  328 |     end case;
  331 |     case opcode is
  332 |       when opcode_alui_c | opcode_lui_c | opcode_auipc_c | opcode_load_c | opcode_store_c | opcode_amo_c | opcode_branch_c | opcode_jal_c | opcode_jalr_c =>
  333 |         ctrl_nxt.alu_opb_mux <= '1';
  334 |       when others =>
  335 |         ctrl_nxt.alu_opb_mux <= '0';
  336 |     end case;
  339 |     if RISCV_ISA_Zaamo and (opcode(2) = opcode_amo_c(2)) and (exe_engine.ir(instr_funct5_lsb_c+1) = '0') then
  340 |       ctrl_nxt.lsu_amo <= '1';
  341 |       ctrl_nxt.lsu_rw  <= '0';
  342 |     elsif RISCV_ISA_Zalrsc and (opcode(2) = opcode_amo_c(2)) and (exe_engine.ir(instr_funct5_lsb_c+1) = '1') then
  343 |       ctrl_nxt.lsu_amo <= '1';
  344 |       ctrl_nxt.lsu_rw  <= exe_engine.ir(instr_funct5_lsb_c);
  345 |     else
  346 |       ctrl_nxt.lsu_amo <= '0';
  347 |       ctrl_nxt.lsu_rw  <= exe_engine.ir(instr_opcode_lsb_c+5);
  348 |     end if;
  351 |     case exe_engine.state is
  353 |       when EX_DISPATCH =>
  355 |         ctrl_nxt.alu_opa_mux <= '1';
  356 |         ctrl_nxt.alu_opb_mux <= '1';
  358 |         if (trap_ctrl.env_pending = '1') or (trap_ctrl.exc_fire = '1') then
  359 |           exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_TRAP_ENTER;
  360 |         elsif RISCV_ISA_Sdtrig and (hwtrig_match = '1') then
  361 |           exe_engine_nxt.pc    <= exe_engine.pc2(XLEN-1 downto 1) & '0';
  362 |           hwtrig_start         <= '1';
  363 |           exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_DISPATCH;
  364 |         elsif (frontend_i.valid = '1') then
  365 |           if_ack               <= '1';
  366 |           trap_ctrl.instr_be   <= frontend_i.fault;
  367 |           exe_engine_nxt.ci    <= frontend_i.compr;
  368 |           exe_engine_nxt.ir    <= frontend_i.instr;
  369 |           exe_engine_nxt.pc    <= exe_engine.pc2(XLEN-1 downto 1) & '0';
  370 |           exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_EXECUTE;
  371 |         end if;
  373 |       when EX_TRAP_ENTER =>
  375 |         if (trap_ctrl.cause(5) = '1') and RISCV_ISA_Sdext then
  376 |           exe_engine_nxt.pc2 <= DEBUG_PARK_ADDR(XLEN-1 downto 2) & "00";
  377 |         elsif (debug_ctrl.run = '1') and RISCV_ISA_Sdext then
  378 |           exe_engine_nxt.pc2 <= DEBUG_EXC_ADDR(XLEN-1 downto 2) & "00";
  379 |         else
  380 |           if (csr.mtvec(0) = '1') and (trap_ctrl.cause(6) = '1') then
  381 |             exe_engine_nxt.pc2 <= csr.mtvec(XLEN-1 downto 7) & trap_ctrl.cause(4 downto 0) & "00";
  382 |           else
  383 |             exe_engine_nxt.pc2 <= csr.mtvec(XLEN-1 downto 2) & "00";
  384 |           end if;
  385 |         end if;
  387 |         if (trap_ctrl.env_pending = '1') then
  388 |           trap_ctrl.env_enter  <= '1';
  389 |           exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_RESTART;
  390 |         end if;
  392 |       when EX_TRAP_EXIT =>
  394 |         if (debug_ctrl.run = '1') and RISCV_ISA_Sdext then
  395 |           exe_engine_nxt.pc2 <= csr.dpc(XLEN-1 downto 1) & '0';
  396 |         else
  397 |           exe_engine_nxt.pc2 <= csr.mepc(XLEN-1 downto 1) & '0';
  398 |         end if;
  399 |         trap_ctrl.env_exit   <= '1';
  400 |         exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_RESTART;
  402 |       when EX_RESTART =>
  404 |         ctrl_nxt.rf_zero_we  <= not bool_to_ulogic_f(CPU_RF_HW_RST_EN);
  405 |         if_reset             <= '1';
  406 |         exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_BRANCHED;
  408 |       when EX_EXECUTE =>
  410 |         exe_engine_nxt.pc2 <= alu_add_i(XLEN-1 downto 1) & '0';
  411 |         trap_ctrl.instr_be <= pmp_fault_i;
  414 |         case opcode is
  417 |           when opcode_alu_c | opcode_alui_c =>
  420 |             case funct3_v is
  421 |               when funct3_sadd_c => ctrl_nxt.alu_op <= alu_op_add_c;
  422 |               when funct3_slt_c  => ctrl_nxt.alu_op <= alu_op_slt_c;
  423 |               when funct3_sltu_c => ctrl_nxt.alu_op <= alu_op_slt_c;
  424 |               when funct3_xor_c  => ctrl_nxt.alu_op <= alu_op_xor_c;
  425 |               when funct3_or_c   => ctrl_nxt.alu_op <= alu_op_or_c;
  426 |               when funct3_and_c  => ctrl_nxt.alu_op <= alu_op_and_c;
  427 |               when others        => ctrl_nxt.alu_op <= alu_op_zero_c;
  428 |             end case;
  431 |             if (funct3_v(2 downto 1) = funct3_slt_c(2 downto 1)) or
  432 |                ((funct3_v = funct3_sadd_c) and (opcode(5) = '1') and (exe_engine.ir(instr_funct7_msb_c-1) = '1')) then
  433 |               ctrl_nxt.alu_sub <= '1';
  434 |             end if;
  437 |             if ((opcode(5) = '0') and (funct3_v /= funct3_sll_c) and (funct3_v /= funct3_sr_c)) or
  438 |                ((opcode(5) = '1') and (((funct3_v = funct3_sadd_c) and (funct7_v = "0000000")) or ((funct3_v = funct3_sadd_c) and (funct7_v = "0100000")) or
  439 |                                        ((funct3_v = funct3_slt_c)  and (funct7_v = "0000000")) or ((funct3_v = funct3_sltu_c) and (funct7_v = "0000000")) or
  440 |                                        ((funct3_v = funct3_xor_c)  and (funct7_v = "0000000")) or ((funct3_v = funct3_or_c)   and (funct7_v = "0000000")) or
  441 |                                        ((funct3_v = funct3_and_c)  and (funct7_v = "0000000")))) then
  442 |               ctrl_nxt.rf_wb_en    <= '1';
  443 |               exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_DISPATCH;
  444 |             else
  445 |               ctrl_nxt.alu_cp_alu  <= '1';
  446 |               exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_ALU_WAIT;
  447 |             end if;
  450 |           when opcode_lui_c =>
  451 |             ctrl_nxt.alu_op      <= alu_op_movb_c;
  452 |             ctrl_nxt.rf_wb_en    <= '1';
  453 |             exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_DISPATCH;
  456 |           when opcode_auipc_c =>
  457 |             ctrl_nxt.alu_op      <= alu_op_add_c;
  458 |             ctrl_nxt.rf_wb_en    <= '1';
  459 |             exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_DISPATCH;
  462 |           when opcode_load_c | opcode_store_c | opcode_amo_c =>
  463 |             exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_MEM_REQ;
  466 |           when opcode_branch_c | opcode_jal_c | opcode_jalr_c =>
  467 |             exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_BRANCH;
  470 |           when opcode_fence_c =>
  471 |             if (exe_engine.ir(instr_funct3_msb_c downto instr_funct3_lsb_c) = funct3_fencei_c) then
  472 |               ctrl_nxt.if_fence    <= '1';
  473 |               exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_RESTART;
  474 |             elsif (exe_engine.ir(instr_funct3_msb_c downto instr_funct3_lsb_c) = funct3_fence_c) then
  475 |               ctrl_nxt.lsu_fence   <= '1';
  476 |               exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_DISPATCH;
  477 |             else
  478 |               exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_DISPATCH;
  479 |             end if;
  482 |           when opcode_fop_c =>
  483 |             ctrl_nxt.alu_cp_fpu  <= '1';
  484 |             exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_ALU_WAIT;
  487 |           when opcode_cust0_c | opcode_cust1_c =>
  488 |             ctrl_nxt.alu_cp_cfu  <= '1';
  489 |             exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_ALU_WAIT;
  492 |           when others =>
  493 |             if ((funct3_v = funct3_csrrw_c) or (funct3_v = funct3_csrrwi_c)) and (exe_engine.ir(instr_rd_msb_c downto instr_rd_lsb_c) = "00000") then
  494 |               csr.re_nxt <= '0';
  495 |             else
  496 |               csr.re_nxt <= '1';
  497 |             end if;
  498 |             exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_SYSTEM;
  500 |         end case;
  502 |       when EX_ALU_WAIT =>
  504 |         ctrl_nxt.alu_op <= alu_op_cp_c;
  505 |         if (alu_cp_done_i = '1') or (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_illegal_c) = '1') then
  506 |           ctrl_nxt.rf_wb_en    <= '1';
  507 |           exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_DISPATCH;
  508 |         end if;
  510 |       when EX_BRANCH =>
  512 |         exe_engine_nxt.ra  <= exe_engine.pc2(XLEN-1 downto 1) & '0';
  513 |         ctrl_nxt.rf_wb_en  <= opcode(2);
  514 |         trap_ctrl.instr_ma <= alu_add_i(1) and branch_taken and bool_to_ulogic_f(not RISCV_ISA_C);
  515 |         if (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_illegal_c) = '0') and (branch_taken = '1') then
  516 |           if_reset             <= '1';
  517 |           exe_engine_nxt.pc2   <= alu_add_i(XLEN-1 downto 1) & '0';
  518 |           exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_BRANCHED;
  519 |         else
  520 |           exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_DISPATCH;
  521 |         end if;
  523 |       when EX_BRANCHED =>
  525 |         exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_DISPATCH;
  527 |       when EX_MEM_REQ =>
  529 |         if (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_illegal_c) = '0') then
  530 |           ctrl_nxt.lsu_req <= '1';
  531 |         end if;
  532 |         exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_MEM_RSP;
  534 |       when EX_MEM_RSP =>
  536 |         if (lsu_wait_i = '0') or
  537 |            (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_saccess_c) = '1') or (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_laccess_c) = '1') or
  538 |            (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_salign_c)  = '1') or (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_lalign_c)  = '1') or
  539 |            (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_illegal_c) = '1') then
  540 |           ctrl_nxt.rf_wb_en    <= (not ctrl.lsu_rw) or ctrl.lsu_amo;
  541 |           exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_DISPATCH;
  542 |         end if;
  544 |       when EX_SLEEP =>
  546 |         if (trap_ctrl.wakeup = '1') then
  547 |           exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_DISPATCH;
  548 |         end if;
  550 |       when others =>
  552 |         exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_DISPATCH;
  553 |         if (funct3_v = funct3_env_c) and (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_illegal_c) = '0') then
  554 |           case exe_engine.ir(instr_funct12_lsb_c+2 downto instr_funct12_lsb_c) is
  555 |             when "000"  => trap_ctrl.ecall      <= '1';
  556 |             when "001"  => trap_ctrl.ebreak     <= '1';
  557 |             when "010"  => exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_TRAP_EXIT;
  558 |             when "101"  => exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_SLEEP;
  559 |             when others => exe_engine_nxt.state <= EX_DISPATCH;
  560 |           end case;
  561 |         end if;
  563 |         if (funct3_v = funct3_csrrw_c) or (funct3_v = funct3_csrrwi_c) or (exe_engine.ir(instr_rs1_msb_c downto instr_rs1_lsb_c) /= "00000") then
  564 |           csr.we_nxt <= '1';
  565 |         end if;
  567 |         ctrl_nxt.rf_wb_en <= '1';
  569 |     end case;
  570 |   end process execute_engine_fsm_comb;
  577 |   ctrl_o.if_fence     <= ctrl.if_fence;
  578 |   ctrl_o.if_reset     <= if_reset;
  579 |   ctrl_o.if_ack       <= if_ack;
  581 |   ctrl_o.pc_cur       <= exe_engine.pc(XLEN-1 downto 1) & '0';
  582 |   ctrl_o.pc_nxt       <= exe_engine.pc2(XLEN-1 downto 1) & '0';
  583 |   ctrl_o.pc_ret       <= exe_engine.ra(XLEN-1 downto 1) & '0';
  585 |   ctrl_o.rf_wb_en     <= ctrl.rf_wb_en and (not trap_ctrl.exc_fire);
  586 |   ctrl_o.rf_rs1       <= exe_engine.ir(instr_rs1_msb_c downto instr_rs1_lsb_c);
  587 |   ctrl_o.rf_rs2       <= exe_engine.ir(instr_rs2_msb_c downto instr_rs2_lsb_c);
  588 |   ctrl_o.rf_rd        <= exe_engine.ir(instr_rd_msb_c downto instr_rd_lsb_c);
  589 |   ctrl_o.rf_zero_we   <= ctrl.rf_zero_we;
  591 |   ctrl_o.alu_op       <= ctrl.alu_op;
  592 |   ctrl_o.alu_sub      <= ctrl.alu_sub;
  593 |   ctrl_o.alu_opa_mux  <= ctrl.alu_opa_mux;
  594 |   ctrl_o.alu_opb_mux  <= ctrl.alu_opb_mux;
  595 |   ctrl_o.alu_unsigned <= ctrl.alu_unsigned;
  596 |   ctrl_o.alu_imm      <= immediate;
  597 |   ctrl_o.alu_cp_alu   <= ctrl.alu_cp_alu;
  598 |   ctrl_o.alu_cp_cfu   <= ctrl.alu_cp_cfu;
  599 |   ctrl_o.alu_cp_fpu   <= ctrl.alu_cp_fpu;
  601 |   ctrl_o.lsu_req      <= ctrl.lsu_req;
  602 |   ctrl_o.lsu_rw       <= ctrl.lsu_rw;
  603 |   ctrl_o.lsu_amo      <= ctrl.lsu_amo;
  604 |   ctrl_o.lsu_mo_we    <= '1' when (exe_engine.state = EX_MEM_REQ) else '0';
  605 |   ctrl_o.lsu_fence    <= ctrl.lsu_fence;
  606 |   ctrl_o.lsu_priv     <= csr.mstatus_mpp when (csr.mstatus_mprv = '1') else csr.prv_level_eff;
  608 |   ctrl_o.csr_we       <= csr.we;
  609 |   ctrl_o.csr_re       <= csr.re;
  610 |   ctrl_o.csr_addr     <= csr.addr;
  611 |   ctrl_o.csr_wdata    <= csr.wdata;
  612 |   ctrl_o.cnt_halt     <= csr.mcountinhibit;
  613 |   ctrl_o.cnt_event    <= cnt_event;
  615 |   ctrl_o.ir_funct3    <= exe_engine.ir(instr_funct3_msb_c downto instr_funct3_lsb_c);
  616 |   ctrl_o.ir_funct12   <= exe_engine.ir(instr_funct12_msb_c downto instr_funct12_lsb_c);
  617 |   ctrl_o.ir_opcode    <= opcode;
  619 |   ctrl_o.cpu_priv     <= csr.prv_level_eff;
  620 |   ctrl_o.cpu_sleep    <= sleep_mode;
  621 |   ctrl_o.cpu_trap     <= trap_ctrl.env_enter;
  622 |   ctrl_o.cpu_debug    <= debug_ctrl.run;
  631 |   multi_cycle_monitor: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
  632 |   begin
  633 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
  634 |       monitor_cnt <= (others => '0');
  635 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
  636 |       if (exe_engine.state = EX_ALU_WAIT) then
  637 |         monitor_cnt <= std_ulogic_vector(unsigned(monitor_cnt) + 1);
  638 |       else
  639 |         monitor_cnt <= (others => '0');
  640 |       end if;
  641 |     end if;
  642 |   end process multi_cycle_monitor;
  645 |   monitor_exc <= monitor_cnt(monitor_cnt'left);
  650 |   csr_check: process(exe_engine.ir, debug_ctrl.run, csr)
  651 |     variable csr_addr_v : std_ulogic_vector(11 downto 0);
  652 |   begin
  654 |     csr_addr_v := exe_engine.ir(instr_imm12_msb_c downto instr_imm12_lsb_c);
  659 |     case csr_addr_v is
  662 |       when csr_cfureg0_c | csr_cfureg1_c | csr_cfureg2_c | csr_cfureg3_c =>
  663 |         csr_valid(2) <= bool_to_ulogic_f(RISCV_ISA_Zxcfu);
  666 |       when csr_fflags_c | csr_frm_c | csr_fcsr_c =>
  667 |         csr_valid(2) <= bool_to_ulogic_f(RISCV_ISA_Zfinx);
  670 |       when csr_mstatus_c  | csr_mstatush_c      | csr_misa_c      | csr_mie_c       | csr_mtvec_c  |
  671 |            csr_mscratch_c | csr_mepc_c          | csr_mcause_c    | csr_mip_c       | csr_mtval_c  |
  672 |            csr_mtinst_c   | csr_mcountinhibit_c | csr_mvendorid_c | csr_marchid_c   | csr_mimpid_c |
  673 |            csr_mhartid_c  | csr_mconfigptr_c    | csr_mxisa_c     | csr_mxiccsreg_c | csr_mxiccdata_c =>
  674 |         csr_valid(2) <= '1';
  677 |       when csr_mcounteren_c | csr_menvcfg_c | csr_menvcfgh_c =>
  678 |         csr_valid(2) <= bool_to_ulogic_f(RISCV_ISA_U);
  681 |       when csr_pmpcfg0_c   | csr_pmpcfg1_c   | csr_pmpcfg2_c   | csr_pmpcfg3_c   |
  682 |            csr_pmpaddr0_c  | csr_pmpaddr1_c  | csr_pmpaddr2_c  | csr_pmpaddr3_c  |
  683 |            csr_pmpaddr4_c  | csr_pmpaddr5_c  | csr_pmpaddr6_c  | csr_pmpaddr7_c  |
  684 |            csr_pmpaddr8_c  | csr_pmpaddr9_c  | csr_pmpaddr10_c | csr_pmpaddr11_c |
  685 |            csr_pmpaddr12_c | csr_pmpaddr13_c | csr_pmpaddr14_c | csr_pmpaddr15_c =>
  686 |         csr_valid(2) <= bool_to_ulogic_f(RISCV_ISA_Smpmp);
  689 |       when csr_mhpmcounter3_c   | csr_mhpmcounter4_c   | csr_mhpmcounter5_c   | csr_mhpmcounter6_c   | csr_mhpmcounter7_c   |
  690 |            csr_mhpmcounter8_c   | csr_mhpmcounter9_c   | csr_mhpmcounter10_c  | csr_mhpmcounter11_c  | csr_mhpmcounter12_c  |
  691 |            csr_mhpmcounter13_c  | csr_mhpmcounter14_c  | csr_mhpmcounter15_c  |
  692 |            csr_mhpmcounter3h_c  | csr_mhpmcounter4h_c  | csr_mhpmcounter5h_c  | csr_mhpmcounter6h_c  | csr_mhpmcounter7h_c  |
  693 |            csr_mhpmcounter8h_c  | csr_mhpmcounter9h_c  | csr_mhpmcounter10h_c | csr_mhpmcounter11h_c | csr_mhpmcounter12h_c |
  694 |            csr_mhpmcounter13h_c | csr_mhpmcounter14h_c | csr_mhpmcounter15h_c |
  695 |            csr_mhpmevent3_c     | csr_mhpmevent4_c     | csr_mhpmevent5_c     | csr_mhpmevent6_c     | csr_mhpmevent7_c     |
  696 |            csr_mhpmevent8_c     | csr_mhpmevent9_c     | csr_mhpmevent10_c    | csr_mhpmevent11_c    | csr_mhpmevent12_c    |
  697 |            csr_mhpmevent13_c    | csr_mhpmevent14_c    | csr_mhpmevent15_c =>
  698 |         csr_valid(2) <= bool_to_ulogic_f(RISCV_ISA_Zihpm);
  701 |       when csr_cycle_c | csr_mcycle_c | csr_instret_c | csr_minstret_c | csr_cycleh_c | csr_mcycleh_c | csr_instreth_c | csr_minstreth_c =>
  702 |         csr_valid(2) <= bool_to_ulogic_f(RISCV_ISA_Zicntr);
  705 |       when csr_dcsr_c | csr_dpc_c | csr_dscratch0_c =>
  706 |         csr_valid(2) <= bool_to_ulogic_f(RISCV_ISA_Sdext);
  709 |       when csr_tselect_c | csr_tdata1_c | csr_tdata2_c | csr_tinfo_c =>
  710 |         csr_valid(2) <= bool_to_ulogic_f(RISCV_ISA_Sdtrig);
  713 |       when others =>
  714 |         csr_valid(2) <= '0';
  716 |     end case;
  721 |     if (csr_addr_v(11 downto 10) = "11") and
  722 |        ((exe_engine.ir(instr_funct3_msb_c downto instr_funct3_lsb_c) = funct3_csrrw_c)  or
  723 |         (exe_engine.ir(instr_funct3_msb_c downto instr_funct3_lsb_c) = funct3_csrrwi_c) or
  724 |         (exe_engine.ir(instr_rs1_msb_c downto instr_rs1_lsb_c) /= "00000")) then
  725 |       csr_valid(1) <= '0';
  726 |     else
  727 |       csr_valid(1) <= '1';
  728 |     end if;
  733 |     if (csr_addr_v(11 downto 4) = csr_dcsr_c(11 downto 4)) and
  734 |        RISCV_ISA_Sdext and (debug_ctrl.run = '0') then
  735 |       csr_valid(0) <= '0';
  736 |     elsif RISCV_ISA_Zicntr and RISCV_ISA_U and (csr.prv_level_eff = '0') and
  737 |           (csr_addr_v(11 downto 8) = csr_cycle_c(11 downto 8)) and
  738 |           (((csr_addr_v(1 downto 0) = csr_cycle_c(1 downto 0)) and (csr.mcounteren_cy = '0')) or
  739 |            ((csr_addr_v(1 downto 0) = csr_instret_c(1 downto 0)) and (csr.mcounteren_ir = '0'))) then
  740 |       csr_valid(0) <= '0';
  741 |     elsif (csr_addr_v(9 downto 8) /= "00") and (csr.prv_level_eff = '0') then
  742 |       csr_valid(0) <= '0';
  743 |     else
  744 |       csr_valid(0) <= '1';
  745 |     end if;
  747 |   end process csr_check;
  752 |   illegal_check: process(exe_engine, csr, csr_valid, debug_ctrl)
  753 |   begin
  754 |     illegal_cmd <= '1';
  755 |     case exe_engine.ir(instr_opcode_msb_c downto instr_opcode_lsb_c) is
  757 |       when opcode_lui_c | opcode_auipc_c | opcode_jal_c =>
  758 |         illegal_cmd <= '0';
  760 |       when opcode_jalr_c =>
  761 |         if (exe_engine.ir(instr_funct3_msb_c downto instr_funct3_lsb_c) = "000") then
  762 |           illegal_cmd <= '0';
  763 |         end if;
  765 |       when opcode_branch_c =>
  766 |         case exe_engine.ir(instr_funct3_msb_c downto instr_funct3_lsb_c) is
  767 |           when funct3_beq_c | funct3_bne_c | funct3_blt_c | funct3_bge_c | funct3_bltu_c | funct3_bgeu_c => illegal_cmd <= '0';
  768 |           when others => illegal_cmd <= '1';
  769 |         end case;
  771 |       when opcode_load_c =>
  772 |         case exe_engine.ir(instr_funct3_msb_c downto instr_funct3_lsb_c) is
  773 |           when funct3_lb_c | funct3_lh_c | funct3_lw_c | funct3_lbu_c | funct3_lhu_c => illegal_cmd <= '0';
  774 |           when others => illegal_cmd <= '1';
  775 |         end case;
  777 |       when opcode_store_c =>
  778 |         case exe_engine.ir(instr_funct3_msb_c downto instr_funct3_lsb_c) is
  779 |           when funct3_sb_c | funct3_sh_c | funct3_sw_c => illegal_cmd <= '0';
  780 |           when others => illegal_cmd <= '1';
  781 |         end case;
  783 |       when opcode_amo_c =>
  784 |         if (exe_engine.ir(instr_funct3_msb_c downto instr_funct3_lsb_c) = "010") then
  785 |           case exe_engine.ir(instr_funct5_msb_c downto instr_funct5_lsb_c) is
  786 |             when "00001" | "00000" | "00100" | "01100" | "01000" | "10000" | "10100" | "11000" | "11100" => illegal_cmd <= not bool_to_ulogic_f(RISCV_ISA_Zaamo);
  787 |             when "00010" | "00011" => illegal_cmd <= not bool_to_ulogic_f(RISCV_ISA_Zalrsc);
  788 |             when others => illegal_cmd <= '1';
  789 |           end case;
  790 |         end if;
  792 |       when opcode_alu_c | opcode_alui_c | opcode_fop_c | opcode_cust0_c | opcode_cust1_c =>
  793 |         illegal_cmd <= '0';
  795 |       when opcode_fence_c =>
  796 |         if (exe_engine.ir(instr_funct3_msb_c downto instr_funct3_lsb_c+1) = funct3_fence_c(2 downto 1)) then
  797 |           illegal_cmd <= '0';
  798 |         end if;
  800 |       when opcode_system_c =>
  801 |         if (exe_engine.ir(instr_funct3_msb_c downto instr_funct3_lsb_c) = funct3_env_c) then
  802 |           if (exe_engine.ir(instr_rs1_msb_c downto instr_rs1_lsb_c) = "00000") and (exe_engine.ir(instr_rd_msb_c downto instr_rd_lsb_c) = "00000") then
  803 |             case exe_engine.ir(instr_funct12_msb_c downto instr_funct12_lsb_c) is
  804 |               when funct12_ecall_c  => illegal_cmd <= '0';
  805 |               when funct12_ebreak_c => illegal_cmd <= '0';
  806 |               when funct12_mret_c   => illegal_cmd <= (not csr.prv_level) or debug_ctrl.run;
  807 |               when funct12_dret_c   => illegal_cmd <= not debug_ctrl.run;
  808 |               when funct12_wfi_c    => illegal_cmd <= (not csr.prv_level) and csr.mstatus_tw;
  809 |               when others           => illegal_cmd <= '1';
  810 |             end case;
  811 |           end if;
  812 |         elsif (csr_valid = "111") and (exe_engine.ir(instr_funct3_msb_c downto instr_funct3_lsb_c) /= funct3_csril_c) then
  813 |           illegal_cmd <= '0';
  814 |         end if;
  816 |       when others =>
  817 |         illegal_cmd <= '1';
  819 |     end case;
  820 |   end process illegal_check;
  825 |   trap_ctrl.instr_il <= '1' when ((exe_engine.state = EX_EXECUTE) or (exe_engine.state = EX_ALU_WAIT)) and
  826 |                                  ((monitor_exc = '1') or (illegal_cmd = '1')) else '0';
  835 |   trap_buffer: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
  836 |   begin
  837 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
  838 |       trap_ctrl.irq_pnd <= (others => '0');
  839 |       trap_ctrl.irq_buf <= (others => '0');
  840 |       trap_ctrl.exc_buf <= (others => '0');
  841 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
  849 |       trap_ctrl.irq_pnd(irq_msi_irq_c) <= irq_machine_i(0);
  850 |       trap_ctrl.irq_pnd(irq_mei_irq_c) <= irq_machine_i(1);
  851 |       trap_ctrl.irq_pnd(irq_mti_irq_c) <= irq_machine_i(2);
  854 |       trap_ctrl.irq_pnd(irq_firq_15_c downto irq_firq_0_c) <= irq_fast_i(15 downto 0);
  857 |       trap_ctrl.irq_pnd(irq_db_halt_c) <= '0';
  858 |       trap_ctrl.irq_pnd(irq_db_step_c) <= '0';
  867 |       trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_msi_irq_c) <= (trap_ctrl.irq_pnd(irq_msi_irq_c) and csr.mie_msi) or (trap_ctrl.env_pending and trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_msi_irq_c));
  868 |       trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_mei_irq_c) <= (trap_ctrl.irq_pnd(irq_mei_irq_c) and csr.mie_mei) or (trap_ctrl.env_pending and trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_mei_irq_c));
  869 |       trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_mti_irq_c) <= (trap_ctrl.irq_pnd(irq_mti_irq_c) and csr.mie_mti) or (trap_ctrl.env_pending and trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_mti_irq_c));
  872 |       for i in 0 to 15 loop
  873 |         trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_firq_0_c+i) <= (trap_ctrl.irq_pnd(irq_firq_0_c+i) and csr.mie_firq(i)) or (trap_ctrl.env_pending and trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_firq_0_c+i));
  874 |       end loop;
  877 |       trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_db_halt_c) <= debug_ctrl.trig_halt or (trap_ctrl.env_pending and trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_db_halt_c));
  878 |       trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_db_step_c) <= debug_ctrl.trig_step or (trap_ctrl.env_pending and trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_db_step_c));
  888 |       trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_lalign_c) <= (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_lalign_c) or lsu_err_i(0))       and (not trap_ctrl.env_enter);
  889 |       trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_salign_c) <= (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_salign_c) or lsu_err_i(2))       and (not trap_ctrl.env_enter);
  890 |       trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_ialign_c) <= (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_ialign_c) or trap_ctrl.instr_ma) and (not trap_ctrl.env_enter);
  893 |       trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_laccess_c) <= (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_laccess_c) or lsu_err_i(1))       and (not trap_ctrl.env_enter);
  894 |       trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_saccess_c) <= (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_saccess_c) or lsu_err_i(3))       and (not trap_ctrl.env_enter);
  895 |       trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_iaccess_c) <= (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_iaccess_c) or trap_ctrl.instr_be) and (not trap_ctrl.env_enter);
  898 |       trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_ecall_c)   <= (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_ecall_c)   or trap_ctrl.ecall)    and (not trap_ctrl.env_enter);
  899 |       trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_illegal_c) <= (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_illegal_c) or trap_ctrl.instr_il) and (not trap_ctrl.env_enter);
  902 |       if RISCV_ISA_Sdext then
  903 |         trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_ebreak_c) <= (not trap_ctrl.env_enter) and (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_ebreak_c) or
  904 |           (trap_ctrl.ebreak and (    csr.prv_level) and (not csr.dcsr_ebreakm) and (not debug_ctrl.run)) or
  905 |           (trap_ctrl.ebreak and (not csr.prv_level) and (not csr.dcsr_ebreaku) and (not debug_ctrl.run)));
  906 |       else
  907 |         trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_ebreak_c) <= (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_ebreak_c) or trap_ctrl.ebreak) and (not trap_ctrl.env_enter);
  908 |       end if;
  911 |       trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_db_break_c) <= (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_db_break_c) or debug_ctrl.trig_break) and (not trap_ctrl.env_enter);
  912 |       trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_db_hw_c)    <= (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_db_hw_c)    or debug_ctrl.trig_hw)    and (not trap_ctrl.env_enter);
  913 |     end if;
  914 |   end process trap_buffer;
  919 |   trap_priority: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
  920 |   begin
  921 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
  922 |       trap_ctrl.cause <= (others => '0');
  923 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
  924 |       trap_ctrl.cause <= (others => '0');
  926 |       if    (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_iaccess_c)  = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_iaf_c;
  927 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_illegal_c)  = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_iil_c;
  928 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_ialign_c)   = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_ima_c;
  929 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_ecall_c)    = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_env_c(6 downto 2) & replicate_f(csr.prv_level, 2);
  930 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_ebreak_c)   = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_brk_c;
  931 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_salign_c)   = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_sma_c;
  932 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_lalign_c)   = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_lma_c;
  933 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_saccess_c)  = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_saf_c;
  934 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_laccess_c)  = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_laf_c;
  936 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_db_halt_c)  = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_db_halt_c;
  937 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_db_hw_c)    = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_db_trig_c;
  938 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_db_break_c) = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_db_break_c;
  939 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_db_step_c)  = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_db_step_c;
  941 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_firq_0_c)   = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_firq0_c;
  942 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_firq_1_c)   = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_firq1_c;
  943 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_firq_2_c)   = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_firq2_c;
  944 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_firq_3_c)   = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_firq3_c;
  945 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_firq_4_c)   = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_firq4_c;
  946 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_firq_5_c)   = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_firq5_c;
  947 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_firq_6_c)   = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_firq6_c;
  948 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_firq_7_c)   = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_firq7_c;
  949 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_firq_8_c)   = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_firq8_c;
  950 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_firq_9_c)   = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_firq9_c;
  951 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_firq_10_c)  = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_firq10_c;
  952 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_firq_11_c)  = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_firq11_c;
  953 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_firq_12_c)  = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_firq12_c;
  954 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_firq_13_c)  = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_firq13_c;
  955 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_firq_14_c)  = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_firq14_c;
  956 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_firq_15_c)  = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_firq15_c;
  958 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_mei_irq_c)  = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_mei_c;
  959 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_msi_irq_c)  = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_msi_c;
  960 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_mti_irq_c)  = '1') then trap_ctrl.cause <= trap_mti_c;
  961 |       end if;
  962 |     end if;
  963 |   end process trap_priority;
  966 |   trap_ctrl.epc <= exe_engine.pc2 when (trap_ctrl.cause(trap_ctrl.cause'left) = '1') else exe_engine.pc;
  971 |   trap_controller: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
  972 |   begin
  973 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
  974 |       trap_ctrl.env_pending <= '0';
  975 |       trap_ctrl.env_entered <= '0';
  976 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
  978 |       if (trap_ctrl.env_pending = '0') then
  979 |         if (trap_ctrl.exc_fire = '1') or (or_reduce_f(trap_ctrl.irq_fire) = '1') then
  980 |           trap_ctrl.env_pending <= '1';
  981 |         end if;
  982 |       else
  983 |         if (trap_ctrl.env_enter = '1') then
  984 |           trap_ctrl.env_pending <= '0';
  985 |         end if;
  986 |       end if;
  988 |       if (exe_engine.state = EX_EXECUTE) then
  989 |         trap_ctrl.env_entered <= '0';
  990 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.env_enter = '1') then
  991 |         trap_ctrl.env_entered <= '1';
  992 |       end if;
  993 |     end if;
  994 |   end process trap_controller;
  997 |   trap_ctrl.exc_fire <= '1' when (or_reduce_f(trap_ctrl.exc_buf) = '1') else '0';
 1000 |   trap_ctrl.irq_fire(0) <= '1' when
 1001 |     (exe_engine.state = EX_EXECUTE) and
 1002 |     (or_reduce_f(trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_firq_15_c downto irq_msi_irq_c)) = '1') and
 1003 |     ((csr.mstatus_mie = '1') or (csr.prv_level = priv_mode_u_c)) and
 1004 |     (debug_ctrl.run = '0') and (csr.dcsr_step = '0') else '0';
 1007 |   trap_ctrl.irq_fire(1) <= '1' when
 1008 |     ((exe_engine.state = EX_EXECUTE) or (exe_engine.state = EX_BRANCHED)) and
 1009 |     (trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_db_halt_c) = '1') else '0';
 1012 |   trap_ctrl.irq_fire(2) <= '1' when
 1013 |     ((exe_engine.state = EX_EXECUTE) or
 1014 |      ((trap_ctrl.env_entered = '1') and (exe_engine.state = EX_BRANCHED))) and
 1015 |     (trap_ctrl.irq_buf(irq_db_step_c) = '1') else '0';
 1020 |   sleep_control: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
 1021 |   begin
 1022 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
 1023 |       sleep_mode <= '0';
 1024 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
 1025 |       if (exe_engine.state = EX_SLEEP) and
 1026 |          (frontend_i.halted = '1') and
 1027 |          (trap_ctrl.wakeup = '0') then
 1028 |         sleep_mode <= '1';
 1029 |       else
 1030 |         sleep_mode <= '0';
 1031 |       end if;
 1032 |     end if;
 1033 |   end process sleep_control;
 1036 |   trap_ctrl.wakeup <= or_reduce_f(trap_ctrl.irq_buf) or debug_ctrl.run or csr.dcsr_step;
 1045 |   csr_addr_reg: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
 1046 |   begin
 1047 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
 1048 |       csr.addr <= (others => '0');
 1049 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
 1050 |       if (opcode = opcode_system_c) then
 1051 |         csr.addr <= exe_engine.ir(instr_imm12_msb_c downto instr_imm12_lsb_c);
 1052 |       end if;
 1053 |     end if;
 1054 |   end process csr_addr_reg;
 1059 |   csr.operand <= rf_rs1_i when (exe_engine.ir(instr_funct3_msb_c) = '0') else (x"000000" & "000" & exe_engine.ir(19 downto 15));
 1062 |   with exe_engine.ir(instr_funct3_msb_c-1 downto instr_funct3_lsb_c) select csr.wdata <=
 1063 |     csr.rdata or       csr.operand  when "10",
 1064 |     csr.rdata and (not csr.operand) when "11",
 1065 |     csr.operand                     when others;
 1070 |   csr_write_access: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
 1071 |   begin
 1072 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
 1073 |       csr.we             <= '0';

 1105 |       csr.we <= csr.we_nxt and (not trap_ctrl.exc_buf(exc_illegal_c));
 1110 |       if (csr.we = '1') then

 1122 |             csr.mstatus_mprv <= csr.wdata(17);
 1123 |             csr.mstatus_tw   <= csr.wdata(21);

 1131 |           csr.mie_mei  <= csr.wdata(11);
 1132 |           csr.mie_firq <= csr.wdata(31 downto 16);
 1133 |         end if;
 1136 |         if (csr.addr = csr_mtvec_c) then

 1153 |         end if;

 1157 |           csr.mepc <= csr.wdata(XLEN-1 downto 1) & '0';

 1160 |           end if;
 1161 |         end if;
 1164 |         if (csr.addr = csr_mcause_c) then

 1166 |         end if;

 1190 |           csr.dcsr_step    <= csr.wdata(2);

 1218 |           end if;

 1222 |         if (csr.addr = csr_tdata2_c) and RISCV_ISA_Sdtrig then
 1223 |           if (debug_ctrl.run = '1') then

 1226 |         end if;
 1231 |       elsif (trap_ctrl.env_enter = '1') then
 1234 |         if (not RISCV_ISA_Sdext) or ((trap_ctrl.cause(5) = '0') and (debug_ctrl.run = '0')) then

 1302 |       if not RISCV_ISA_Zicntr then

 1350 |   csr.prv_level_eff <= priv_mode_m_c when (debug_ctrl.run = '1') else csr.prv_level;
 1355 |   csr_read_access: process(rstn_i, clk_i)

 1448 |           when csr_mip_c =>
